Conclusion: VCDP作为一种轻量级、即插即用的训练正则化方法，有效提升了半监督3D医学图像分割在复杂解剖结构上的性能，且不增加推理负担。

Abstract: Semi-supervised 3D medical image segmentation reduces the need for dense voxel-level annotations by exploiting unlabeled volumes. Although existing methods such as consistency regularization, pseudo-labeling, and co-training improve prediction-level robustness, they often provide insufficient feature-space organization for anatomically complex structures, especially small organs and ambiguous boundary regions with large intra-class variations. To address this issue, we propose Variation-Conditioned Distributional Proxy Learning (VCDP), a plug-and-play training-only regularization module for semi-supervised 3D medical image segmentation. VCDP represents each class with a learnable Gaussian distribution for shared class semantics and multiple variation prototypes for fine-grained intra-class patterns. A unified variation-conditioned compatibility score is further formulated to fuse distributional similarity and soft variation aggregation, guiding voxel embeddings to align with both global organ identity and local anatomical variations. VCDP is attached to decoder features during training and removed during inference, introducing no additional inference cost. Experiments on multi-organ segmentation benchmarks show that VCDP improves most evaluated baselines, particularly for small, ambiguous, and highly variable organs. Our anonymous code is released at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/VCDP_code-41ED.

</details>


### [71] [Two-Stage Multi-Modal Fusion with Adaptive Alignment for Action Quality Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07438)
*Kanglei Zhou,Ruizhi Cai,Xinning Wang,Yijian Zheng,Liyuan Wang,Jianguo Li,Xiaohui Liang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DualAlign，一种两阶段多模态融合框架，通过自适应对齐先构建视觉表征再融入文本语义，解决了动作质量评估中跨模态不对齐和标注稀缺的问题。在自建的MM-JDM数据集及RG、Fis-V基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AQA方法面临两个主要挑战：1）异构模态间存在跨模态不对齐和不稳定融合问题；2）可靠的多模态标注成本高，导致数据集多样性受限。

Method: 提出DualAlign两阶段融合框架：第一阶段通过最大化RGB视频、光流和骨架模态间的共享结构信息，构建一致的视觉表征；第二阶段在视觉稳定后融入文本语义，使高层描述补充而非扭曲底层视觉流形。同时构建包含RGB、光流、骨架和结构化文本的MM-JDM数据集以评估真实多模态条件。

Result: 在MM-JDM上平均相关性提升21.16%，在RG和Fis-V基准上分别提升3.53%和5.95%，且在模态缺失和标签稀缺条件下保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 两阶段自适应对齐策略能有效解决多模态融合中的不对齐问题，且构建的MM-JDM数据集为多模态AQA研究提供了更具挑战性的基准。

Abstract: Action Quality Assessment (AQA) aims to evaluate how well a person performs a movement, which is essential in applications such as sports scoring, skill assessment, and healthcare. However, unimodal approaches often struggle to capture subtle cues of movement quality in real-world settings. Although multi-modal inputs provide complementary information, existing methods still face two major challenges: heterogeneous modalities often lead to cross-modal misalignment and unstable fusion, and reliable multi-modal annotation is costly, resulting in limited dataset diversity. To address these challenges, we propose DualAlign, a two-stage multi-modal fusion framework with adaptive alignment. The framework first constructs a coherent visual representation by maximizing shared structural information across RGB video, optical flow, and skeleton modalities. Textual semantics are then incorporated after visual stabilization, allowing high-level descriptions to complement rather than distort the underlying visual manifold. To evaluate the framework under realistic multi-modal conditions, we introduce MM--JDM, a movement-quality assessment dataset integrating RGB videos, optical flow, skeleton sequences, and structured text. MM--JDM naturally exhibits modality noise, class imbalance, and label scarcity, making it a challenging benchmark for studying multi-modal fusion and alignment. Extensive experiments show that DualAlign improves average correlation on MM--JDM by 21.16% over the state-of-the-art methods and achieves gains of 3.53% and 5.95% on the RG and Fis-V benchmarks, respectively. DualAlign also remains robust under missing-modality and label-scarce conditions.

</details>


### [72] [A Theory of Contrastive Learning with Natural Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07470)
*Antonio Torralba,Yair Weiss*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文通过理论分析证明，在简单图像和增强条件下，对比学习的最优表示可通过第一层为正弦滤波器、后接非线性激活、全局平均池化和部分白化线性层的CNN实现，并给出了基于功率谱的水填充算法来计算最优参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究对比学习为何能在简单图像和增强条件下产生有用的下游任务表示，从理论角度解释其有效性。

Method: 通过解析计算对比学习损失下的最优表示，理论分析CNN第一层滤波器为正弦波的最优性；提出基于数据集期望功率谱的水填充算法计算正弦波频率和权重；通过SGD训练实验验证理论结果。

Result: 对于特定增强方式，最优CNN第一层滤波器是正弦波，后接非线性、全局平均池化和部分白化线性层；更复杂增强下最优权重仍为正弦波；实验表明SGD训练的CNN确实学到了正弦波和部分白化。

Conclusion: 对比学习的有效性可归结为学习正弦滤波器组和部分白化过程，为理解对比学习提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: Why does contrastive learning with simple images and augmentations yield useful representations for downstream tasks? We address this question by analytically computing the optimal representation in terms of a contrastive loss for a range of basic augmentations and any image dataset with stationary statistics. We show that for certain augmentations the optimum can be attained by a CNN whose first layer filters are sinusoids, followed by a pointwise nonlinearity, global average pooling, and a final linear layer that performs partial whitening. We also show that the optimal weights in such CNNs for more complicated augmentations are still sinusoids. The frequencies of the sinusoids and their weights can be computed using a simple waterfilling algorithm given the dataset's expected power spectrum. Experiments with different image datasets and augmentations show that such CNNs trained with SGD empirically learn sinusoids in their first layer and to perform partial whitening

</details>


### [73] [Discovering Geometric Biases in 3D Face Reconstruction: A Curvature-Aware Spectral Framework for Fairness Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07486)
*Veronika Shilova,Emmanuel Malherbe,Giovanni Palma,Panagiotis-Alexandros Bokaris,Laurent Risser,Jean-Michel Loubes*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于曲面曲率的3DMM重建偏差分析框架，利用拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米算子生成高分辨率曲率误差图，揭示了年龄、性别和种族相关的系统性偏差，比传统欧氏距离指标更符合人类感知。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DMM从有限的人脸样本中学习，继承了训练数据中的形态偏差，可能限制其在全球多样化人群中的泛化能力。传统欧氏距离评估指标无法捕捉细微的曲面特征偏差。

Method: 利用拉普拉斯-贝尔特拉米算子(LBO)生成高分辨率曲率误差图，提供局部化且几何意义鲜明的偏差可视化；从中导出新的误差度量，并通过用户研究验证其与人类感知的相关性。

Result: 新误差度量与人类感知的相关性显著高于传统方法；在多种3DMM基和拟合算法上发现了系统性的年龄相关偏差，并初步发现了性别和种族相关的偏差。

Conclusion: 未来3D人脸重建研究应采用曲率感知的评估协议，以确保人口统计公平性和几何精度。

Abstract: 3D Morphable Models (3DMMs) remain the standard parametric shape priors for many state-of-the-art 3D face reconstruction algorithms. However, as these models are derived from a finite number of 3D face samples, they inherit the morphological biases of their training data, potentially limiting their generalizability across diverse global populations. In this paper, we propose a novel framework to analyze 3DMM reconstructions through the lens of surface curvature, with the objective to discover, quantify and visualize biases. While standard evaluation metrics often rely on Euclidean distances, our reconstruction error captures subtle surface nuances such as local topology or undulations. To do so, we leverage the Laplace-Beltrami Operator (LBO) to generate high-resolution curvature error maps, providing a localized and geometrically meaningful visualization of discrepancies between ground truth faces and reconstructed meshes. We derive from it an error metric that we validated through a user study, observing a significantly higher correlation to human perception compared to traditional methods. Furthermore, we conduct extensive experiments across several 3DMM bases and fitting algorithms, uncovering systematic age-related biases and providing preliminary evidence of biases associated with gender and ethnicity. Our findings highlight the necessity of adopting curvature-aware evaluation protocols to ensure demographic fairness and geometric precision in future 3D face reconstruction research.

</details>


### [74] [Learning to Unify Deformable Shape and Texture Representations for Cardiac Video Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07518)
*Tonmoy Hossain,Miaomiao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种双向交叉注意力融合框架，首次在隐空间中联合学习可变形形状和图像纹理的时序特征，并实现心脏相位自适应加权，在心脏磁共振视频分类上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法简单拼接形状与纹理特征，既未充分利用两者的互补性，也未学习跨模态特征依赖关系；同时对所有心脏时相采用统一注意力权重，忽略了不同心脏期相诊断重要性的差异。

Method: 设计双向交叉注意力机制在隐空间融合可变形形状与图像纹理特征，使每种模态基于时空对应关系自适应加权另一种模态；动态调整不同心脏期相的形状与纹理贡献权重。

Result: 在电影心脏磁共振视频数据集上达到SOTA分类性能；注意力机制提升了模型可解释性，能够识别诊断关键的心脏期相及各模态贡献。

Conclusion: 跨模态隐空间融合与时间自适应加权策略有效提升了心脏视频分类性能与可解释性，验证了形状-纹理特征互补融合的潜力。

Abstract: Deformable shape representations have proven to be robust complements to texture features in cardiac image classification, offering geometric priors that are invariant to imaging artifacts and intensity variations. However, existing deep networks perform simple concatenation to combine these distinct feature representations, which neither fully exploits their complementary nature nor learns cross-modal feature dependencies. Furthermore, this results in uniform attention across all timepoints; hence ignoring the varying diagnostic importance across the cardiac phases. In this paper, we propose a novel cardiac video classification model that, for the first time, learns temporal features in an integrated space of deformable shape and image texture representations. In particular, we design a bi-directional cross-attention in the latent space to fuse latent deformable shape and image features, allowing each modality to adaptively weight the other based on spatio-temporal correspondence. In contrast to current methods that apply uniform weighting across all the cardiac phases, our approach learns to dynamically adjust the contributions of shape and texture representations, derived from images, over time. We demonstrate state-of-the-art classification performance on a cine cardiac magnetic resonance (CMR) video dataset, achieving improved interpretability from attention mechanisms that identify diagnostically critical cardiac phases and modality contributions.

</details>


### [75] [Context-Aware Slum Mapping in Sub-Saharan Africa Using Sentinel-1 Texture and Local Climate Zones](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07532)
*Peterson Chepkilot,Babak Memar,Paolo Gamba*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种结合光学和SAR影像的上下文感知框架，通过Sentinel-1/2数据提升撒哈拉以南非洲非正规住区（LCZ 7）的识别精度，总体精度达0.816（旱季）和0.807（雨季），显著优于WUDAPT基线（0.704），并将LCZ 3与LCZ 7的混淆率降至7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撒哈拉以南非洲城市中，光学影像难以区分非正规住区（LCZ 7）与光谱相似的紧凑低层建筑区（LCZ 3），现有方法精度不足，亟需更可靠的制图方案。

Method: 提出光学-SAR融合框架：使用Sentinel-2光谱特征与Sentinel-1结构信息，采用三层次SAR集成策略（校正后向散射、GLCM纹理、物理引导特征），并基于改进的局部气候区（LCZ）分类体系进行非正规住区识别。

Result: SAR纹理特征对LCZ 7检测贡献最大；光学-SAR模型总体精度0.816（旱季）/0.807（雨季），优于WUDAPT基线（0.704）；LCZ 3与LCZ 7的混淆率降至7%；SAR纹理可稳定跨季节制图，但跨城市迁移性有限。

Conclusion: 融合SAR特征能显著提升数据稀缺环境下城市形态制图的一致性和跨季节稳定性，但跨城市泛化仍需本地化适配策略。

Abstract: Accurate mapping of informal settlements remains a major challenge in Sub-Saharan African (SSA) cities because optical imagery often fails to distinguish Informal Settlements (defined here as LCZ 7) from spectrally similar formal Compact Low-Rise areas (LCZ 3). This study presents a context-aware, reproducible Optical-SAR framework that improves informal settlement delineation using Sentinel-2 spectral features and Sentinel-1 structural information within an adapted Local Climate Zone (LCZ) taxonomy. We implement a three-tier SAR integration strategy: calibrated backscatter, GLCM textures, and a physics-guided feature engineered to capture the high structural disorder and weak radar return characteristic of SSA informal settlements. Using reference data across Nairobi and Eldoret (Kenya), we evaluate performance via a stratified hold-out protocol and a season-aware ablation study. Results show that SAR textures provide the dominant performance gain for LCZ 7 detection. The Optical-SAR model achieves overall accuracy of 0.816 (dry) and 0.807 (wet), significantly outperforming the WUDAPT baseline (OA 0.704) and reducing the critical LCZ 3 - LCZ 7 confusion to 7%. Seasonal analysis indicates that while optical-only separability varies with phenology, SAR-derived textures stabilize informal settlement mapping across seasons. These findings demonstrate that the incorporation of SAR-derived features yields consistent improvements for urban morphology mapping in data-scarce environments across seasons and across the evaluated source cities, while cross-city transfer remains limited without local adaptation strategies.

</details>


### [76] [Infinite Worlds with Versatile Interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07534)
*Zelin Gao,Qiuyu Wang,Jiapeng Zhu,Jingye Chen,Zichen Liu,Qingyan Bai,Jiahao Wang,Yufeng Yuan,Hanlin Wang,Yichong Lu,Ka Leong Cheng,Haojie Zhang,Jian Gao,Tianrui Feng,Yuzheng Liu,Yao Yao,Yinghao Xu,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Hao Ouyang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LingBot-World 2.0 是一个升级版的世界模拟器，支持无限交互、60fps实时响应、多样化交互元素，并首次引入双代理架构（驾驶员+导演），支持多玩家体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提升世界模拟器的交互时长、实时性、交互多样性，并引入智能体协同机制来增强世界建模能力。

Method: (1) 因果预训练范式实现无限交互；(2) 从基模型蒸馏出实时变体；(3) 增加多种动作和文本驱动事件；(4) 引入驾驶员代理（规划行为）和导演代理（生成环境元素）的双代理架构。

Result: 支持720p 60fps实时视频流；14B主模型+1.3B轻量模型可单GPU部署；支持多玩家同时在线互动。

Conclusion: LingBot-World 2.0 在交互时长、实时性、多样性和代理协同方面实现了显著进步，为沉浸式世界模拟提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: We present LingBot-World 2.0 (also known as LingBot-World-Infinity), an advanced iteration of LingBot-World featuring four distinct upgrades. (1) Our model achieves an unbounded interaction horizon while maintaining consistent output quality, benefiting from a carefully crafted causal pretraining paradigm. (2) Through distilling a real-time variant from the base model, our system guarantees rapid response time, sufficient to drive 720p video streams at 60 fps. (3) Compared to the previous version, this update introduces highly diverse interactive elements, comprising a broader spectrum of actions (e.g., attacking, archery, spell-casting, and shooting) alongside a richer variety of text-driven events. (4) We pioneer the integration of an agentic harness within the domain of world modeling, wherein a pilot agent is tasked with planning and executing character behaviors, while a director agent is responsible for synthesizing novel environmental elements as the scene progresses. Additionally, to facilitate a shared experience, we develop an interface that permits multiple players to simultaneously immerse themselves in this vivid world simulator. We pair our primary 14B model with a lightweight 1.3B counterpart, which supports effortless deployment on a single GPU.

</details>


### [77] [Face-trace: Open-Set Attribution and Progressive Discovery of Synthetic Face Generators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07545)
*Alessia Infantino,Claudio Schiavella,Irene Amerini*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向合成人脸图像的开集溯源管线，结合已知生成器分类、能量基OOD拒绝和未知生成器聚类发现，在WILD数据集上实现了96.73%闭集准确率和71.25%开集平衡准确率，聚类纯度达87.74%，增量场景下纯度可达99.23%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有合成人脸溯源方法大多假设闭集场景（所有生成器在训练时已知），但现实中新生成器不断涌现，被拒绝样本应被组织归类而非简单丢弃，因此需要开集溯源方案。

Method: 使用冻结I-JEPA嵌入训练已知生成器分类器，结合能量基OOD检测拒绝未知样本；将拒绝样本的投影I-JEPA特征与法医自描述符拼接后聚类，发现未知生成器分组；并扩展至增量场景处理随时间到达的拒绝样本。

Result: WILD数据集上闭集准确率96.73%；开集能量基拒绝平衡准确率71.25%；未知生成器聚类ARI 0.81、NMI 0.90、纯度87.74%；增量场景最终纯度99.23%。跨数据集实验表明管线可超越原始数据分布，但后处理仍具挑战。

Conclusion: 提出了一种有效的开集合成人脸溯源管线，在已知分类、未知拒绝与聚类发现及增量扩展方面均表现良好，但跨数据集泛化中的后处理仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: Recent advances in generative Artificial Intelligence have made synthetic face images increasingly realistic, creating new challenges for multimedia forensics. Source attribution methods should not only identify the generator of an image when the source is known, but also handle samples produced by previously unseen models. However, most existing approaches address synthetic face attribution in a closed-set setting, where all possible generators are available during training. This assumption does not hold in real-world scenarios, where new generators continuously appear and rejected samples should be organized rather than simply discarded. In this work we propose a pipeline for open-set synthetic face source attribution that combines known generator classification, energy-based OOD rejection, and unknown generator discovery. A classifier is trained on known generators using frozen I-JEPA embeddings, while rejected samples are represented by combining projected I-JEPA features with Forensic Self-Descriptors and then clustered to discover groups of unknown generators. We also extend the discovery stage to an incremental scenario, where rejected samples arrive over time. Experiments on the WILD dataset show that the proposed method achieves 96.73% closed-set attribution accuracy. In the open-set setting, energy-based rejection reaches 71.25% balanced accuracy, while rejected samples are clustered into meaningful unknown-generator groups, obtaining an ARI of 0.81, an NMI of 0.90, and an overall clustering purity of 87.74%. In the incremental setting, the discovered generator space is progressively extended while maintaining a final purity of 99.23%. Cross-dataset experiments suggest that the pipeline can operate beyond the original dataset distribution, although post-processing remains challenging.

</details>


### [78] [AA-ViT: Anatomically Aware Vision Transformer with Structural and Frequency Guidance for Contrast Enhanced Brain MRI Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07553)
*Talha Meraj,Tom Flannery,Charlie Cummins,Matt Townend,Thomas C Booth,Peter Crossley,Michael McCann,Ian Overton,Saritha Unnikrishnan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出AA-ViT，一个解剖感知的频率与结构引导的视觉Transformer，用于无创合成对比增强MRI（CEMRI），在BraTS 2021数据集上取得了优于现有方法的PSNR和SSIM，并获得了三位神经放射科医生和一位神经外科医生的临床验证（平均评分3.94/5）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准MRI对比度有限且存在伪影，而化学对比剂并非总是可用（如肾功能不全患者），因此需要开发无创且准确的CEMRI合成方法。现有生成式AI方法依赖隐式特征学习，难以保留解剖边界和肿瘤精细结构。

Method: 提出解剖感知的频率与结构引导视觉Transformer（AA-ViT），利用预对比MRI模态（T1、T2、FLAIR）合成CEMRI，通过频率和结构引导机制增强解剖边界和病灶结构的保留能力。

Result: 在BraTS 2021数据集上，PSNR和SSIM均优于现有最先进方法；三位神经放射科医生与一位神经外科医生对19例多样化胶质瘤病例的临床评估平均得分为3.94/5。

Conclusion: AA-ViT能有效合成高质量CEMRI，降低扫描成本、缩短成像时间，并避免钆基对比剂的潜在风险，且获得了初步临床验证。

Abstract: Accurate tumour localization and diagnosis is a critical component of clinical care for brain cancers. Magnetic Resonance Imaging (MRI) is the most commonly used imaging modality due to its superior soft-tissue contrast. However, standard MRI often exhibits limited contrast and imaging artifacts, which necessitates the use of contrast agents to enhance lesion visibility. The administration of chemical contrast agents is not always feasible and may be contraindicated in patients with renal impairment or other health conditions. As a result, developing accurate and non-invasive contrast enhanced MRI (CEMRI) synthesis methods has clinical importance. In recent years, numerous approaches for CEMRI synthesis have been proposed, predominantly relying on generative artificial intelligence models. While these methods demonstrate promising performance, their dependence on implicit feature learning often limits their ability to preserve anatomical boundaries and tumour-specific fine structures. To address these challenges, we propose an anatomically aware frequency-and-structure-guided vision transformer (AA-ViT), for CEMRI synthesis using pre-contrast MRI modalities (T1, T2, and FLAIR). Experiments on the BraTS 2021 dataset demonstrate that the proposed method preserves anatomical and lesion boundaries, achieving higher PSNR and SSIM than state-of-the-art approaches. Clinical evaluation by three neuroradiologists and a neurosurgeon on 19 randomly selected cases across diverse gliomas yielded a mean score of 3.94/5, providing preliminary clinical validation rarely seen in prior studies. Synthetic post-contrast scans from our model could lower scanning costs, shorten imaging time, and avoid the potential risks of using gadolinium-based contrast agents.

</details>


### [79] [Automatic Echocardiography Segmentation via Transition Probability Correlation for Stable Semantic Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07580)
*Xinran Chen,Xiyuan Wang,Guangquan Zhou,Chuan Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STLSF模块（含语义校正与纹理增强）及频率感知去噪预训练方法，构建卷积网络，在超声心动图分割任务上达到SOTA性能（CAMUS Dice 93.87%，EchoNet-Dynamic Dice 92.62%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超声心动图存在斑点噪声和低信噪比，导致语义特征模糊、边界不完整，限制了深度学习模型在复杂临床案例中的分割精度；同时心脏的时序运动对识别解剖结构至关重要。

Method: 1) 设计STLSF模块，包含窗口匹配语义校正组件（利用局部转移概率相关性校正语义）和语义引导纹理增强组件；2) 提出频率感知去噪预训练方法，帮助编码器适应超声成像模式的内在先验；3) 构建基于卷积的网络，兼具局部归纳偏置与长程依赖。

Result: CAMUS数据集Dice 93.87%、HD95 3.29mm；EchoNet-Dynamic数据集Dice 92.62%、HD95 2.73mm，达到SOTA。

Conclusion: STLSF模块有效缓解了超声图像质量不佳导致的纹理不稳定和语义歧义问题，频率感知预训练进一步提升了编码器对超声成像模式的适应性，整体方法在两项基准上取得最优分割性能。

Abstract: While echocardiography is essential for cardiovascular diagnosis, inherent speckle noise and low signal-to-noise ratio often lead to ambiguous semantic features and fragmented boundaries. These limitations significantly hinder the segmentation accuracy of deep learning models in complex clinical cases. Moreover, temporal motion of the heart plays a critical role in recognizing anatomical structures. To address these challenges, we designed a STLSF module which comprises a window-matching-based semantic correction component and a semantics-guided texture enhancement component. By leveraging local transition probability correlations to correct semantics and employing semantics-guided texture enhancement, the STLSF module effectively mitigates texture instability and ambiguous semantic interpretations caused by disadvantaged echocardiography quality. Additionally, to facilitate the encoder's adaptation to the intrinsic priors of ultrasound-specific imaging patterns, we propose a frequency-aware denoising pre-training method. The entire work builds a convolution-based network with locality inductive bias and long-range dependencies. Extensive experiments confirm our SOTA performance, achieving 93.87\% Dice on CAMUS and 92.62\% on EchoNet-Dynamic, with respective HD95 values of 3.29mm and 2.73mm.

</details>


### [80] [Cardiac MRI Through-Plane Super-Resolution Guided by Reference and Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07581)
*Shaoming Pan,Chenchuhui Hu,Leon Axel,Meng Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出STRMSR框架，利用同一受试者的高分辨率参考视图和中间超分辨率结果作为记忆，通过粗到细的上下文匹配和可学习的动态特征聚合模块，实现心脏MRI的穿平面超分辨率重建，在4x和8x上采样下优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床心脏MRI为缩短扫描时间、适应屏气和心脏运动约束，通常采用高面内分辨率但粗穿平面分辨率，限制了3D分析和诊断准确性，因此需要穿平面超分辨率技术。

Method: 提出STRMSR框架：1）利用同一受试者的HR参考视图和中间SR结果作为记忆引导重建；2）粗到细的上下文匹配建立低分辨率目标与参考/记忆图像之间的鲁棒对应关系；3）可学习的逐块动态特征聚合模块预测自适应混合权重，融合动态信息并抑制不可靠特征迁移；4）记忆库存储中间SR结果确保切片间一致性。

Result: 在WHS心脏MRI数据集上，在正交平面视图和长轴腔视图两种参考协议下，4x和8x上采样因子均一致优于基线方法。

Conclusion: STRMSR通过参考和记忆引导有效提升心脏MRI穿平面分辨率，保证3D体积的切片间一致性，为临床心脏成像的3D分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Clinical cardiac MRI is commonly acquired with high in-plane resolution but coarse through-plane resolution to reduce scan time and accommodate breath-hold and cardiac-motion constraints, which limits 3D analysis and diagnostic accuracy. We propose STRMSR, a reference- and memory-guided through-plane super-resolution (SR) framework that reconstructs high-resolution (HR) cardiac volumes by leveraging HR reference views acquired from the same subject and intermediate SR results as the memory. Our method uses coarse-to-fine contextual matching to establish robust correspondence between low-resolution target and reference/memory images under spatial misalignment. A learnable patch-wise dynamic feature aggregation module predicts content-adaptive mixture weights for each local patch, effectively fusing dynamic information while suppressing unreliable feature transfers. The intermediate SR results stored in the memory bank ensure slice-to-slice consistency for the super-resolved 3D volume. Experiments on the WHS cardiac MRI dataset under two reference protocols, orthogonal-plane views and long-axis chamber views, demonstrate consistent improvements over baselines at 4x and 8x upsampling factors.

</details>


### [81] [MedPMC: A Systematic Framework for Scaling High-Fidelity Medical Multimodal Data for Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07673)
*Hyunjae Kim,Dain Kim,Pan Xiao,Serina S. Applebaum,Younjoon Chung,Xuguang Ai,Yu Yin,Roy Jiang,Yuexi Du,Yawen Wei,Yiming Kong,Tuo Guo,Zhiyuan Cao,Mengmeng Du,Yuelei Fu,Yan Hu,Rui Shi,Gui Yang,Kevin W. Jin,Yuntian Liu,Yuxuan Tian,Jonathan Marquez,Zhen Chen,Sheng Zhang,Hoifung Poon,Hua Xu,Jaewoo Kang,Qingyu Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MedPMC是一个自动化、可持续更新的框架，从PMC的610万篇文章中筛选出1100万高质量医学图像-文本对。经训练的多模态模型在26项基准测试、医学视觉问答和临床皮肤科图像检索中均显著优于现有方法，且95.3%的图像经人工评估具有医学相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医学多模态基础模型的发展受限于大规模高质量临床数据的获取困难。虽然PMC提供了专业的图像-文本数据，但现有PMC衍生数据集在保真度、可复现性和临床验证方面存在严重不足。

Method: 提出了MedPMC自动化框架，对610万篇PMC文章进行流水线处理：初始筛选（F1=93.2）、多面板图检测（F1=96.5）、图分离（mAP=89.8）、标题分离与对齐（F1=81.4; ROUGE-L=85.3）以及医学图像分类（F1=96.5），最终得到1100万对高质量医学图像-文本对。

Result: 1) 95.3%的图像经人工审查具有医学相关性（对比先前PMC数据集的19.7%）；2) CLIP-style模型在26项基准测试中AUC平均提升7.1个百分点；3) 医学VQA提升1.9-16.9个百分点；4) 临床皮肤科图像检索Recall@5提升11.7个百分点。

Conclusion: 高保真度的文献筛选能够显著增强医学多模态基础模型在基准测试和临床环境中的性能。框架、语料库、基准和预训练模型均已公开。

Abstract: Medicine is inherently multimodal, requiring clinicians to synthesize information across diverse data streams. Yet the development of multimodal foundation models is constrained by limited access to large-scale, high-quality clinical data. Although PubMed Central (PMC) offers a complementary source of expert-authored image-text data, existing PMC-derived resources remain limited in fidelity, reproducibility, and clinical validation. We introduce MedPMC, an automated, continuously updatable framework that transforms permissively licensed literature into high-fidelity infrastructure for medical multimodal models. Applied to 6.1 million PMC articles, MedPMC curated 11 million medical image-text pairs. Component evaluations showed strong performance for initial screening (F1 = 93.2), multi-panel figure detection (F1 = 96.5), figure separation (mAP = 89.8), caption separation and alignment (F1 = 81.4; ROUGE-L = 85.3), and medical figure classification (F1 = 96.5). Manual review by five annotators, three with medical training, found 95.3% of MedPMC images medically relevant, versus 19.7% in a prior PMC-derived dataset. Across 26 benchmarks spanning 11 specialties, a MedPMC-trained CLIP-style model improved average zero-shot AUC by 7.1 percentage points over the strongest architecture-matched biomedical CLIP baseline despite using fewer than half as many image-text pairs. As the vision encoder in a multimodal large language model, it improved medical visual question-answering by 1.9 and 16.9 percentage points across two benchmarks. In 10,524 Yale New Haven Health System dermatology photographs, it improved morphology-to-image retrieval Recall@5 by 11.7 percentage points. These findings show that high-fidelity literature curation strengthens medical multimodal foundation models across benchmark and clinical settings. We publicly release the framework, corpus, benchmarks, and pretrained models.

</details>


### [82] [Scaling Mixture-of-Experts Video Pretraining for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07675)
*Shuailei Ma,Jiaqi Liao,Xinyang Wang,Jingjing Wang,Chaoran Feng,Zijing Hu,Chong Bao,Zichen Xi,Yuqi Gan,Weisen Wang,Yanhong Zeng,Qin Zhao,Zifan Shi,Wei Wu,Hao Ouyang,Qiuyu Wang,Shangzhan Zhang,Jiahao Shao,Yipengjing Sun,Liangxiao Hu,Lunke Pan,Nan Xue,Kecheng Zheng,Yinghao Xu,Xing Zhu,Yujun Shen,Ka Leong Cheng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出LingBot-Video，一个基于DiT和MoE的视频预训练范式，专为具身智能设计，通过机器人数据增强、多维奖励系统等解决视频生成模型在机器人控制中的领域不匹配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型专注于内容创作，追求视觉保真度和创造力，忽略了计算效率和物理真实性，导致在机器人控制任务中存在领域不匹配问题。

Method: 1) 架构上采用MoE（混合专家）框架替代密集模型，实现建模能力与推理效率的平衡；2) 数据上构建数据画像引擎，增强标准互联网视频与大量机器人操作、导航、第一人称视角数据；3) 训练上开发多维奖励系统，加强物理合理性和任务完成度的对齐。

Result: 综合评估验证了其作为视频基础模型的性能和效率，是首个大规模开源MoE视频基础模型。

Conclusion: LingBot-Video作为首个大规模开源MoE视频基础模型，致力于弥合数字创意与物理执行之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Despite the recent promise in robot control, video generative models suffer from a domain mismatch due to their primary focus on content creation. For example, their design inherently prioritizes visual fidelity and creativity over computational efficiency and physical realism. In this work, we present LingBot-Video, a DiT-based video pretraining paradigm specifically tailored for embodied intelligence. From the architecture perspective, we adopt the Mixture-of-Experts (MoE), instead of dense, framework to achieve a better trade-off between modeling capacity and inference efficiency, and manage to scale it up from scratch. From the data perspective, we construct a data profiling engine that augments standard internet videos with extensive robot-oriented footage, encompassing manipulation, navigation, and egocentric perspectives, to equip the base model with an intrinsic understanding of actions and world dynamics. From the training perspective, we develop a multi-dimensional reward system to enforce the alignment regarding physical rationality and task completion, going beyond standard criteria such as aesthetics, prompt-following, and motion consistency. Comprehensive evaluations validate its performance and efficiency as a video foundation model. We contribute LingBot-Video as the inaugural large-scale, open-source MoE video foundation model to the community, in a pioneering effort to bridge digital creativity and physical actuation.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [83] [AgentLens: Production-Assessed Trajectory Reviews for Coding Agent Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06624)
*Andrey Podivilov,Vadim Lomshakov,Sergey Savin,Matvei Startsev,Roman Pozharskiy,Maksim Parshin,Sergey Nikolenko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentLens 是一个面向交互式代码智能体的生产级基准，通过形式化验证与 LLM 评估相结合，全面评估智能体在执行任务过程中的完整轨迹（指令遵循、工具使用、错误恢复等），而非仅以最终通过/失败作为评判标准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码智能体基准将运行结果简化为单一比特（通过/失败），无法反映用户实际体验到的完整轨迹——智能体如何遵循指令、使用工具、验证自身工作、从错误中恢复以及与人交互等关键过程。

Method: AgentLens 采用双重评估机制：一方面进行形式化验证（客观检查），另一方面利用 LLM 进行轨迹审查和成对比较，为每次运行生成可读的评分解释。

Result: AgentLens 不仅可用于模型排名，还能诊断模型行为、比较同一智能体的不同版本，并在夜间评估流水线中捕获产品回归问题。

Conclusion: AgentLens 作为一个开源的生产级基准，为交互式代码智能体提供了更全面、更具可解释性的评估方案，超越了传统的通过/失败二元判断。

Abstract: We present AgentLens, a production-assessed benchmark for interactive code agents. Most code-agent benchmarks reduce a run to a single bit -- did the task pass? -- but the people who actually use these agents experience the entire trajectory: how the agent follows instructions, uses its tools, verifies its own work, recovers from mistakes, and talks to them along the way. AgentLens evaluates that whole trajectory. It pairs formal verification, where an objective check exists, with LLM-written trajectory reviews and side-by-side comparisons, so that each run yields a readable explanation of why the score is what it is. This makes AgentLens useful for more than ranking models: we use it to diagnose model behavior, compare successive versions of our own agent, and catch product regressions in a nightly evaluation pipeline. We release the benchmark as open source at https://github.com/agent-lens/agent-lens-bench.

</details>


### [84] [When Does In-Context Search Help? A Sampling-Complexity Theory of Reflection-Driven Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06720)
*Yotam Wolf,Noam Wies,Amnon Shashua*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文对LLM的上下文搜索（in-context search）进行理论分析，将其建模为推理轨迹上的近似推理，证明了当自我反思能可靠定位早期错误时，上下文搜索可用多项式次数的顺序尝试实现指数级改进，并验证了理论预测在真实推理模型上的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为LLM的上下文搜索（in-context search）行为提供严格的理论分析框架，解释其为何有效以及何时有效。

Method: 将上下文搜索建模为推理轨迹上的近似后验推理：基础模型定义先验，自我反思提供反馈进行后验更新。分析了推理时的采样复杂度，并从强化学习视角给出了最优策略扩展的理论联系。

Result: 1) 当反思能可靠定位早期错误时，上下文搜索可用多项式次数的顺序尝试实现指数级改进；2) 当该性质不成立时，条件采样与并行采样渐进等价；3) 近似后验更新足够，交叉熵训练可用多项式样本恢复所需行为；4) 强化学习的最优策略扩展实现了相同的后验重加权规则。

Conclusion: 上下文搜索的有效性依赖于自我反思定位早期错误的能力，其理论优势可被学习且对近似更新鲁棒，与强化学习框架具有内在一致性。

Abstract: Training large language models (LLMs) with extended reasoning has enabled in-context search, in which models iteratively generate, critique, and revise solution attempts. We provide a theoretical analysis of in-context search by modeling it as approximate inference over reasoning traces, where the base model defines a prior and self-reflection provides feedback for posterior updates, and study the resulting inference-time sampling complexity - the number of sequential attempts needed to achieve high success probability. We show that when reflections reliably localize early mistakes, in-context search can yield exponential improvements over the base model, solving problems with exponentially small zero-shot pass rates using only a polynomial number of sequential attempts, whereas when this property fails, conditioning on past attempts offers no asymptotic benefit over parallel sampling. We further show that these gains are robust and learnable: approximate posterior updates suffice, and cross-entropy training on search rollouts recovers the required behavior with polynomial sample complexity. Finally, we show that under a stagewise abstraction of reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards, the optimal policy extension implements the same posterior reweighting rule. We validate key qualitative predictions of the theory on real large reasoning models.

</details>


### [85] [LLM-powered reasoning in agent-based modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06757)
*Sifat Afroj Moon,Dakotah Maguire,Adam Spannaus,Joe Tuccillo,Maksudul Alam,Sudip K. Seal,John Gounley,Heidi Hanson*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出HALE框架，将大语言模型(LLM)与基于智能体的建模(ABM)相结合，利用LLM预测人类决策行为，使流行病模拟能够适应实时变化，并以COVID-19在盐湖县的模拟作为概念验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ABM依赖静态先验知识，无法适应实时变化，存在信息滞后问题。作者希望利用LLM的预测能力来弥补这一信息缺口，使ABM能够动态响应真实世界的变化。

Method: 提出HALE（Hybrid Agent-based and Language-driven Epidemic）框架，将LLM集成到ABM中，利用LLM预测模拟中智能体的人类决策行为。

Result: 以COVID-19在犹他州盐湖县的模拟作为概念验证，展示了HALE框架的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: HALE框架成功地将LLM的预测能力与ABM的规模化模拟相结合，为克服传统ABM的静态局限提供了新路径，在政策制定和流行病模拟领域具有重要应用前景。

Abstract: Agent-based modeling (ABM) has the capability to model millions of individuals and their interactions, which is useful for policy making. However, ABMs have traditionally relied on static prior, which prevents the models from adapting to real-time changes. Our research provides a novel approach to addressing this information gap. Large language models (LLMs) offer new opportunities to predict human decision-making. Here, we introduce a scalable Hybrid Agent-based and Language-driven Epidemic (HALE) modeling framework that leverages LLMs to predict human decision-making in an ABM simulation. As a proof-of-concept, we use HALE to simulate COVID-19 and its effects in Salt Lake County, UT.

</details>


### [86] [QANTIS: Hardware-Calibrated Sequential POMDP Belief Updates on IBM Heron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06760)
*Bayram Yuksel Eker,Suayb S. Arslan,Ozgur Nazli,Mustafa Serhat Demirgil,Furkan Deligoz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过IBM Heron硬件实验验证了QANTIS框架在部分可观测条件下，利用量子处理器作为信念更新服务，能够在顺序Tiger POMDP任务中保持后验概率的一致性，确保下游动作选择不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在部分可观测的自主系统中，智能体需基于信念（而非原始传感器事件）进行决策。量子处理器可能提供高效的信念更新服务，但需验证其在顺序决策场景中能否保持后验概率的准确性，避免下游规划出错。

Method: 在IBM Heron硬件上，针对Tiger POMDP任务，比较三种放大策略（无放大、保守型Grover放大、全步固定点放大FPAA），检查硬件返回的后验是否改变下游动作。同时使用边界感知BIQAE稳定振幅估计，并通过稀有事件扫描映射样本复杂度包络。

Result: 全步FPAA在8步和12步主运行中保持了Tiger后验一致性，20步和32步控制实验在同一运行区间内。所有决策检查中，硬件后验与精确贝叶斯后验选择相同的即时动作。BIQAE在零和一附近稳定了振幅估计。

Conclusion: 研究给出了硬件校准信念更新原语的可运行包络，而非独立的硬件优势声明。QANTIS在真实量子硬件上可复用作为信念更新服务，不会破坏规划器所需的后验概率。

Abstract: Autonomous systems under partial observability act on beliefs, not raw sensor events. QANTIS treats the quantum processor as a calibrated belief-update service in that loop: it receives a prior and an observation model, estimates the rare-event evidence term, and returns an ordinary posterior to a classical planner. This paper asks whether that service can be reused across a sequential Tiger POMDP horizon on present IBM Heron hardware without corrupting the planner-facing posterior. We answer with a controlled hardware case study rather than an end-to-end autonomy or wall-clock speedup claim. The study compares no amplification, guarded Grover amplification, and all-step fixed-point amplification on the same trajectory, then checks whether the returned posterior would change the downstream action. All-step FPAA preserves the Tiger posterior across the reported 8-step and 12-step primary runs, and the 20-step and 32-step controls remain inside the same operating band. In every reported decision check, the hardware posterior and the exact Bayes posterior select the same immediate action. Boundary-aware BIQAE stabilizes amplitude estimation near zero and near one, while a rare-event sweep maps the logical sample-complexity envelope for one-in-a-million evidence. The result is an operating envelope for a hardware-calibrated belief-update primitive, not a standalone hardware-advantage claim.

</details>


### [87] [Cost-Effective Agent Harnesses for Abstract Reasoning and Generalization on ARC-AGI-1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06764)
*Kabir Moghe,Peter Chin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在不使用基准专用训练或大量测试时间计算的情况下，通过架构设计提升ARC-AGI-1任务性能。提出了Explorer-Definer Pipeline和Reflective Orchestrator两种智能体架构，将模式发现与程序合成分离，并引入反思性探索机制。在公开的400个任务上，管道达到57.50% pass@2（每任务$0.25），协调器达到67.25% pass@2（每任务$0.62），将15.50%的基线提升约52个百分点。通过pass@k分析发现模型是生成受限而非选择受限，协调器通过自适应重探索验证了这一诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ARC-AGI-1的进展主要来自两条路径：一是前沿模型的大量测试时间计算（进化搜索、穷举采样、扩展思维链），二是针对基准的小模型微调。本文探索第三条路径：使用开放权重模型（DeepSeek V3.2）的非思考模式，在严格预算下，不进行特定微调，仅通过架构设计提升性能。

Method: 提出两种架构：1) Explorer-Definer Pipeline：两阶段智能体管道，将模式发现与可执行变换合成显式分离；2) Reflective Orchestrator：在管道基础上增加自主探索机制，当先前假设在训练对上失败时自动探索新变换。同时引入偏置pass@k分析诊断模型的生成-选择边界。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-1公开400任务集上：Pipeline达57.50% pass@2（$0.25/任务），Orchestrator达67.25% pass@2（$0.62/任务），将15.50%基线提升约52个百分点。pass@k分析表明模型是生成受限（generation-bound）而非选择受限（selection-bound），选择可达候选上限的~95%。Orchestrator的自适应重探索验证了此诊断，无偏pass@1提升+9.81 pp。think工具移除导致pass@2下降5.75 pp。

Conclusion: 通过纯粹的架构设计（无微调、无大量测试时计算），可显著提升开放权重模型在ARC-AGI-1上的性能。模型瓶颈在于生成更广泛的候选方案，而非更好的排序选择。反思性协调器通过自适应重探索有效缓解了生成受限问题。

Abstract: Recent progress on ARC-AGI-1 from disclosed architectures has come broadly from two regimes: heavy test-time compute over frontier models (evolutionary search, exhaustive sampling, extended chain-of-thought), or benchmark-specific training in which small models are fine-tuned on ARC data, often with task-specialized architectures. We study a third regime: an open-weight model in non-thinking mode (DeepSeek V3.2) under a strict budget, with no ARC-specific fine-tuning. We study what is recoverable through architecture alone, building agentic harnesses that decompose pattern-discovery and program-synthesis stages explicitly. First, we introduce an Explorer-Definer Pipeline that separates pattern discovery from executable transformation synthesis, implemented as a two-stage agent pipeline. Next, we present the Reflective Orchestrator, which augments the pipeline with autonomous exploration of new transformations when previous hypotheses fail on training pairs. On the ARC-AGI-1 public 400-task evaluation set, the pipeline reaches 57.50% pass@2 at \$0.25 per task, and the orchestrator reaches 67.25% pass@2 at \$0.62 per task. Together these architectures lift a 15.50% one-shot baseline by ~52 points without benchmark-specific training or heavy test-time compute. Furthermore, the orchestrator-driven lift tests a falsifiable diagnostic the pipeline produces; unbiased pass@k analysis suggests the pipeline is generation-bound, not selection-bound (selection via training-pair accuracy captures ~95% of the candidate ceiling) and predicts that significant improvement requires broader generation, not better ranking. The orchestrator implements this prediction via adaptive re-exploration and confirms it (unbiased pass@1 lift +9.81 pp, matching selection-mediated pass@2 lift). An additional pipeline ablation identifies its think tool as a significant component, with removal reducing pass@2 by 5.75 pp.

</details>


### [88] [Evaluating SageMath-Augmented LLM Agents for Computational and Experimental Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06820)
*Pavel Snopov,German Magai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合LLM推理与SageMath计算机代数系统反馈的ReAct风格智能体框架，并通过Context7实现文档检索。在RealMath基准测试中，该方法使所有模型平均性能提升9.7个百分点（最高27.8pp），其中GPT-5.5达到75.2%的最高求解率，同时缩小了开源与闭源模型之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI for Mathematics研究主要聚焦于自动形式化和定理证明，计算机代数系统（CAS）在LLM智能体工作流中的作用尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出ReAct风格智能体框架，结合LLM推理、SageMath可验证反馈及Context7文档检索；改进RealMath基准，引入多步后处理和多阶段验证流程以提升数据质量。

Result: 所有模型平均提升9.7pp（1.5~27.8pp），Qwen 3.7-Max获益最大，GPT-5.5达75.2%最高求解率且token消耗最低，缩小了开源与闭源模型差距。

Conclusion: CAS增强的智能体是辅助数学家计算探索的有前景方向，该工作是迈向自动化猜想发现的一步。

Abstract: Recent advances in AI for Mathematics have focused largely on autoformalization and theorem proving, leaving the role of Computer Algebra Systems (CAS) in agentic LLM workflows underexplored. We propose a ReAct-style agentic setup that combines LLM reasoning with verifiable feedback from SageMath, together with Context7 for the up-to-date documentation. We evaluate this agentic setup across frontier models for solving research-level mathematical problems from the RealMath benchmark in a setting that emulates a computational-mathematics research loop. We also propose a refinement to the RealMath benchmark by introducing a multi-step post-processing procedure and a multi-stage validation pipeline, both of which improve the quality and reliability of the extracted problem set. Our experiments reveal substantial performance gains from SageMath access across all evaluated models on +9.7~pp on average, the gains range from 1.5~pp to 27.8~pp and narrow the gap between open-weight and closed models. Qwen~3.7-Max benefits from SageMath the most, while GPT-5.5 achieves the highest solve rate of $75.2\%$ and the lowest token usage among tool-enabled configurations. Our findings suggest that CAS-augmented agents represent a promising direction for assisting mathematicians in computational exploration, and we believe that this work is a step towards automated conjecture discovery. The project repository is available online.

</details>


### [89] [The Harness Effect: How Orchestration Design Sets the Token Economics of Enterprise Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06906)
*Muayad Sayed Ali,Aliaksandra Novik,Anji Boddupally,Artem Yavorskyi,Chris Nickerson,Daniel Rica,Emily DuGranrut,Felix Leung,Garrett Prince,Grace Barnett,Heath Robinson,Hosain Al Ahmad,Jesse Resnick,Juan Carlos Farah,Jyothi Swaroop Meruga,Leonid Kuznetsov,Luke Gorham,Marie Schmoll,Michael Paciullo,Saumya Das,Sharath Sheripally,Tommy Griscom,Mykyta Osadchyi,Neha Mantri,Nick Westrum,Olivia Benowitz,Parikshith Kulkarni,Radik Chernyshov,Rakshith Vasudev,Rohith Nadimpally,Vikas Gangadevi,Waseem AlShikh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证了AI智能体开发的“Token最大化”问题（任务消耗Token增长快于价值增长），并提出解决方案在于编排层（Harness）。通过控制变量实验（6个模型，仅改动编排层），Writer Agent Harness将每任务成本降低41%、时间减少44%、Token消耗减少38%，同时质量持平甚至略升。编排层对成本的影响超过了模型选择的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI智能体开发存在“Token最大化”现象——为提升能力不断消耗更多Token（更长的推理链、更多轮次、更大的工具负载、更大的上下文），导致每任务的Token消耗增速超过任务价值本身。虽然单Token价格下降掩盖了这一趋势，但总体支出仍在上升。作者认为需要找到控制成本的关键杠杆。

Method: 采用控制变量实验：固定22个评估任务、6个基础模型（Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.1, Gemini Flash 3.5, Qwen 3.6, GLM 5.1, Palmyra X6），仅改变编排层——对比一个固定的传统生产循环与Writer Agent Harness。测量成本、时间、Token消耗和任务完成质量等指标。

Result: Harness将混合每任务成本降低41%（$0.21→$0.12）、中位墙钟时间减少44%（48s→27s）、每任务Token减少38%（14.2k→8.8k），任务完成质量持平（0.78→0.81）。效率提升模型无关（所有模型成本降低33-61%），质量提升与模型基础能力强相关（r=0.99），称为"Harness杠杆"。质量/美元提升82%，每百万Token任务完成数从54.9升至92.0。

Conclusion: 编排层对每任务成本的影响超过了模型菜单中不同模型之间的全部成本差异。作者形式化了编排层的Token经济学（包括提示缓存下的有效输入价格），详细描述了六种机制（从缓存形状规整到失败支出治理），并比较了六种常见智能体系统。论证编排层是唯一一个效率能够乘数倍放大组织运行的所有模型（现在和未来）的组件。

Abstract: Agentic AI development today runs on token maxing: buying capability with tokens -- longer reasoning traces, more turns, wider tool payloads, bigger replayed contexts -- so tokens per task grow faster than task value. Falling per-token prices mask the pattern; total spend rises anyway. We argue the decisive lever against token maxing is the harness: the orchestration layer that assembles context, exposes tools, sequences turns, delegates work, and carries enterprise observability and governance. We isolate it with a controlled swap: 22 locked evaluation tasks, six foundation models (Claude Sonnet 4.6, Gemini 3.1, Gemini Flash 3.5, Qwen 3.6, GLM 5.1, Palmyra X6), changing only the orchestration layer -- a frozen conventional production loop versus the Writer Agent Harness. Holding models constant, the harness cuts blended cost per task 41% ($0.21->$0.12), median wall-clock 44% (48s->27s), and tokens per task 38% (14.2k->8.8k), with task-completion quality at parity (0.78->0.81, directional at this sample size). Efficiency is model-invariant -- every model gets cheaper (33-61%) -- while quality gains are capability-dependent: a model's gain correlates almost perfectly with its baseline strength (r=0.99, n=6), a phenomenon we term harness leverage. Quality per dollar rises 82%; task-completions per million tokens rise from 54.9 to 92.0. On this workload the orchestration layer moved cost per task more than the full spread of the model menu did. We formalize token economics at the orchestration layer (including effective input price under prompt caching), detail the six mechanism families behind the effect -- cache-shape discipline to failure-spend governance -- compare six widely used agent systems on the same axes, and argue the harness is the one component whose efficiency multiplies across every model an organization runs -- present and future.

</details>


### [90] [Grounding Spatial Relations in a Compact World Model: Instruction Leakage and a Goal-Free Dynamics Fix](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06925)
*Yufeng Wang,Lu Wei,Haibin Ling*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现目标条件世界模型中的"指令泄露"问题：模型看似学会将语言指令与场景关联，实则是在转录指令而非真正感知环境。通过多种实验证明这一混杂因素，并提出将目标从动力学中移除、监督读取路径的修复方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目标条件世界模型中语言指令是否真正实现了与视觉场景的接地，识别出指令转录（instruction transcription）这一伪像而非真正感知的陷阱。

Method: 通过三种设置和组内消融实验，采用去掉目标、反事实指令等诊断方法，测试了桌面环境、BabyAI基准和Language-Table前向动力学模型，比较关系读取准确率的变化。

Result: 目标条件预测器达到0.90的关系读取准确率，但这是指令转录而非感知：去掉目标后降至0.27，反事实指令使预测锚点94.5%跟随虚假指令。指令泄露的根本特征是评分量可从指令中转录（即指令命名了答案）。

Conclusion: 诊断揭示了修复方案：将目标从动力学中移除（归入规划器的成本函数），监督读取路径，可恢复真正独立于指令的接地（0.88，有无目标结果相同）。该检测协议和修复方法适用于任意指令命名了评分量的目标条件世界模型。

Abstract: Compact world models that condition on a language goal promise to ground relations such as ``put the red block left of the blue block'' using a sparse set of explicit \emph{reference anchors}. We ask when such references actually ground a relation, and identify a trap: a goal-conditioned predictor reaches a striking $0.90$ relation-readout accuracy, yet this is \emph{instruction transcription}, not perception. Withholding the goal collapses it to chance ($0.90\!\to\!0.27$, three seeds) and a counterfactual instruction makes the predicted anchors follow the \emph{false} instruction $94.5\%$ of the time (true scene $2.3\%$; $N{=}256$). Tested across three settings and a within-task ablation, our central claim characterizes the confound: \textbf{instruction leakage occurs when the scored quantity is transcribable from the instruction (when the instruction names the answer) and is essentially independent of how predictive the non-instruction inputs are.} Our tabletop and the external BabyAI benchmark leak, whereas a Language-Table forward-dynamics world model whose instruction names \emph{referents} does not, until the instruction is augmented to name the direction; and degrading the action never increases leakage, the opposite of what predictor-competition predicts. The diagnosis prescribes the fix: keep the goal out of the dynamics (it belongs to the planner's cost) and supervise the \emph{read} path, recovering genuine, instruction-independent grounding ($0.88$, identical with and without the goal). The detection protocol and remedy apply to any goal-conditioned world model whose instruction names the scored quantity.

</details>


### [91] [Large Behavior Model: A Promptable Digital Twin of the Retail Customer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.06993)
*Wachiravit Modecrua,Krittin Pachtrachai,Touchapon Kraisingkorn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出大型行为模型(LBM)，通过统一的人物-环境框架直接从大规模零售交易中学习客户决策，在多个任务上超越通用语言模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法要么优化预测精度但缺乏可解释性，要么模拟用户但缺乏真实行为数据支撑，需要一种既能准确预测又能解释决策的行为建模方法。

Method: 采用统一的人物-环境框架，客户状态通过历史购买行为档案表示，产品上下文通过检索增强生成引入；训练包括持续预训练、监督微调决策生成、以及基于可验证奖励的强化学习。

Result: 在购买预测、硬负样本判别、购物篮补全、促销响应和跨域券兑换等任务上持续优于前沿通用语言模型，并展现出强大的零样本和微调迁移能力。

Conclusion: 交易历史中的行为知识可以被语言模型有效学习，为客户数字孪生和行为模拟提供可扩展的基础；消融研究表明持续预训练是行为泛化的主要驱动力，检索在训练和推理时同时使用最有效，强化学习能改善对显式行为证据的依赖。

Abstract: Customer behavior modeling underpins recommendation, marketing, and decision support, yet existing approaches either optimize predictive accuracy without explaining decisions or simulate users without grounding them in real behavioral data. We present the Large Behavioral Model (LBM) that learns customer decision making directly from large-scale retail transactions through a unified Person-Environment formulation. Customer state is represented by a behavioral profile derived from historical purchases, while product context is incorporated through retrieval-augmented generation. The model is trained using continued pre-training on verbalized behavioral data, supervised fine-tuning for decision generation, and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards for evidence-based calibration. We evaluate the proposed framework on purchase prediction, hard-negative discrimination, basket completion, promotion response, and cross-domain voucher redemption. The model consistently outperforms frontier general-purpose language models on in-domain retail tasks while demonstrating strong zero-shot and fine-tuned transfer across retailers and decision domains. Ablation studies show that continued pre-training is the primary driver of behavioral generalization, retrieval is most effective when applied during both training and inference, and reinforcement learning improves reliance on explicit behavioral evidence over generic language-model priors. These results demonstrate that behavioral knowledge encoded in transaction histories can be effectively learned by language models, providing a scalable foundation for customer digital twins and behavior simulation.

</details>


### [92] [Learning social norms enhances compatibility in dynamic human-AI coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07021)
*Yi Yang,Siyuan Liu,Xin Gao,Huamu Sun,Chao Liu,Qing Zhou,Bingbing Nie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究人类与AI在动态交互中的协调问题，提出将隐性社会规范显式化为三个可量化原则（结果可预测性、价值对齐、优势意识），并将其融入大语言模型。在行人-车辆交互实验中，社会规范引导的AI相比基线策略总分提升近4倍，甚至超越人类-人类交互表现43%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI模型在与人类动态交互时难以实现有效、自然、体谅的协调，根本原因在于对齐模型行为时未显式量化行为背后的隐性社会规范。

Method: 以行人-车辆交互为代表性场景，构建简化实验平台收集3,456次动态交互数据，从中归纳出三项社会规范原则：结果可预测性、价值对齐、优势意识。将这些原则注入AI智能体进行闭环交互任务测试。

Result: 社会规范引导的LLM总分是基线策略的4倍，比人类-人类交互高43%。

Conclusion: 将隐性社会规范形式化为可量化原则，能显著提升AI在动态交互中实现互利协调的能力，推动AI更自然地融入人类社会。

Abstract: Humans continuously coordinate with others in dynamic interactions, often through implicit, hard-to-quantify social norms that act as shared tacit expectations among interacting agents. As AI agents, including large language models (LLMs), become embedded in daily life, they increasingly participate in such interactions and reshape social interaction structures. Yet they often fail to coordinate with humans in an effective, considerate, and natural manner. We hypothesize that this gap arises because existing approaches align model behavior with human demonstrations without explicitly quantifying the underlying norms that generate such behavior. We selected pedestrian-vehicle interaction as a representative dynamic interaction and developed a simplified experimental platform that captures its key interactive features. From 3,456 dynamic human interactions collected via this platform, we identified three principles underlying human social norms: outcome predictability, value alignment, and advantage awareness. Incorporating these principles into AI agents significantly improves human-AI coordination. In the closed-loop interaction task with humans, the social-norm-informed LLM achieved a nearly fourfold higher total score than the baseline strategy and outperformed human-human interactions by 43%. These findings indicate that formalizing tacit social norms into explicit, quantifiable principles can enable AI agents to achieve mutually beneficial coordination in dynamic interactions, supporting their more natural integration into human society.

</details>


### [93] [Measuring Intelligence Beyond Human Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07040)
*Jerry Han,Rafael Moschopoulos,Ella Colby,Vishrut Goyal,Andrew Tu,Kia Ghods,Mark Braverman,Elad Hazan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于相对测量的对抗性心理测量框架，通过让模型生成公共挑战来相互区分，从而在超越人类能力的情况下继续评估智能系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人类标注的基准测试在模型超越人类能力后失效，因为人类无法知道哪些任务是既困难又可验证的，需要一种能随系统能力扩展的评估范式。

Method: 构建对抗性心理评分系统：模型生成公共挑战来分离其他系统，聚合结果形成相对测量；设计了减少私有信息攻击、无需裁判的裁决协议，可自然扩展。

Result: 该框架在可验证和开放式的非可验证领域均得到实例化，展示了模型生成评估可以持续衡量超越人类前沿的系统。

Conclusion: 绝对尺度评估存在固有限制，相对测量范式是突破人类能力上限的可行路径，为超人类智能评估提供了新方向。

Abstract: How can we measure intelligence beyond human capability?
  Human-authored benchmarks saturate, and above human capability, examiners may not know which tasks are both hard and verifiable. We argue that this difficulty is inherent to absolute-scale evaluation and propose a new paradigm based on relative measurement in which models generate public challenges that separate other systems. Aggregating these outcomes yields an adversarial psychometric rating system that can scale with the systems being measured. We describe practical protocols that reduce incentives for private-information attacks, support judge-free adjudication, and naturally scale with agent capabilities. We instantiate the framework across verifiable and open-ended, non-verifiable domains, illustrating how model-generated evaluation can continue to measure systems beyond the human frontier.

</details>


### [94] [Operational Reframing and Approval-Framed Delegation in Multi-Agent LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07097)
*Lifei Liu,Haoran Yu,Xiaochong Jiang,Su Wang,Pin Qian,Yihang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种五条件对比设计，将多智能体LLM系统的管道安全效应分解为操作重述、规划器行为和委托框架三种机制。实验表明：聚合安全并非稳定的架构属性，操作重述是最具可迁移性的风险信号，规划器可通过拒绝缓解风险但若产生可执行步骤会提升执行者合规性，委托框架受提示设计、模型配对和场景来源影响。原始直接提示的排名无法预测实际部署行为，建议分别报告各机制后再归因于架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究将多智能体LLM系统的"管道效应"作为单一指标报告，但这一聚合值混淆了有害意图被重构为合理工作、规划器拒绝/转换请求、执行者在委托提示下行动三种不同机制，导致难以解释安全差异的真实来源。

Method: 提出五条件受控对比设计（直接提示、操作重述基线、规划器条件、委托框架条件等），在30个合成有害场景和来自四个智能体安全基准的外部验证集上，使用LLM判定的合规性作为评估指标。

Result: 1) 聚合管道安全不是稳定的架构属性；2) 操作重述是最具可迁移性的风险信号，GPT、Gemini和DeepSeek均表现出合规性上升，Claude则具抗性；3) 规划器主要通过拒绝缓解风险，但若能生成可执行步骤，执行者合规性可能超过直接操作基线；4) 委托框架受提示设计、模型配对和场景来源影响显著，怀疑性执行者提示可大幅降低合规性；5) 原始直接提示排名不可预测部署行为，如Gemini在直接提示中最安全但与Claude规划器配对后合规率从8.9%升至38.9%。

Conclusion: 多智能体安全评估应在归因于架构之前，分别报告重述效应、规划器行为、委托框架和模型配对的影响，而非仅报告单一的聚合管道效应。

Abstract: Safety evaluations of multi-agent LLM systems often compare a direct prompt with a planner-executor pipeline and report the difference as a single "pipeline effect." We argue that this aggregate is difficult to interpret because it conflates three mechanisms: harmful intent may be reframed as plausible operational work, the planner may refuse or transform the request, and the executor may act under delegation prompts implying prior approval. To separate these factors, we introduce a five-condition controlled contrast design, evaluated on 30 synthetic harmful scenarios and an exploratory external validation set from four agent-safety benchmarks using LLM-judged compliance.
  Our results show that aggregate pipeline safety is not a stable architectural property. Operational reframing is the most portable risk signal, increasing compliance for GPT, Gemini, and DeepSeek across both scenario sets, while Claude is comparatively resistant. Planner behavior can offset this risk mainly through refusal; however, when the planner produces executable steps, the executor may become more compliant than under the direct operational baseline. Approval-framed delegation is sensitive to prompt design, model pairing, and scenario source, and a skeptical executor prompt sharply reduces compliance.
  Raw-direct model rankings can also mispredict deployed planner-executor behavior. Gemini is safest under raw direct prompts in the primary set yet shows the largest amplification with a Claude planner, rising from 8.9 percent to 38.9 percent compliance. GPTs near-zero aggregate pipeline effect instead hides a reframing increase canceled by planner refusal. These findings suggest that multi-agent safety evaluations should report reframing, planner behavior, delegation framing, and model pairing separately before attributing failures to architecture itself.

</details>


### [95] [Does AI Understand Imaging? A Systematic Benchmark of Agentic AI for Computational Imaging Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07189)
*Ethan Chung,Chuanjun Zheng,Jasper Tan,Jingxi Li,Haopeng Zhang,Huaijin Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ImagingBench基准，包含20个计算成像任务，评估视觉语言模型在物理驱动成像问题上的表现，发现Agentic模型远弱于专用方法，尤其在计算感知任务上差距显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在语义视觉任务上表现优异，但能否处理计算成像中的物理和逆问题尚不明确，亟需一个统一基准来评估这一差距。

Method: 设计了20个跨5类别的计算成像任务（射线/波动光学、图像信号处理、逆重建、计算感知、标定），设置三种评估模式：Expert（固定专家引导逆重建）、Planner（规划器引导逆重建）、Forward（前向仿真一致性检查）。

Result: Agentic模型在所有任务上均弱于专用方法，计算感知任务（无透镜成像、事件重建、飞行时间成像、全息术）差距尤其大；Planner引导相比固定Expert基线提升有限且不稳定；模型虽能生成视觉上合理的输出，但参考保真度很差。

Conclusion: 语义视觉能力与物理驱动成像性能之间存在显著鸿沟，ImagingBench为衡量这一差距并追踪Agentic AI在计算成像领域的进展提供了统一测试平台。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) and agentic AI have shown strong performance on semantic visual tasks, but it remains unclear whether they can handle the physics and inverse problems that underlie computational imaging. We present ImagingBench, a benchmark of 20 computational imaging tasks spanning five categories: ray and wave optics, image signal processing, inverse reconstruction, computational sensing, and calibration. ImagingBench evaluates three complementary settings: Expert, fixed expert-guided inverse reconstruction; Planner, planner-guided inverse reconstruction; and Forward, forward-system simulation for consistency checking. We benchmark leading proprietary and open-source image-centric multimodal systems, including Gemini, GPT, and Qwen, and compare them with representative task-specific non-agentic baselines. Across tasks, agentic models remain consistently weaker than specialized methods, especially on computational sensing problems such as lensless imaging, event-based reconstruction, time-of-flight imaging, and holography. Planner guidance provides only modest and inconsistent gains over the fixed-prompt Expert baseline. Although the models often generate visually plausible outputs, their reference-based fidelity remains poor, revealing a substantial gap between semantic visual competence and physically grounded imaging performance. ImagingBench provides a unified testbed for measuring this gap and tracking progress in agentic AI for computational imaging.

</details>


### [96] [Reasoning Consistency Scanning: A Framework for Auditing Chain-of-Thought Validity in AI Safety Evaluations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07229)
*Silvia Santano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出推理一致性扫描方法，用于在不进行实验干预的情况下，通过检测AI模型推理过程与答案之间的逻辑一致性来评估安全性，并建立了一套六类不一致性子类型体系及基准测试集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究关注链式思维推理的忠实性问题，但检测不忠实需要实验性干预，无法事后应用于评估记录。作者转向更易处理的问题：推理是否与答案逻辑一致，这无需干预即可从记录中评估。

Method: 形式化定义推理一致性与忠实性的区别，建立六类不一致性子类型；构建60条手动标注的基准测试集；实现首个针对安全评估记录中推理一致性的扫描器（InspectScout）；在4个生成模型和3个评估任务上报告结果。

Result: 推理不一致现象确实存在、可检测，并且在不同模型和任务类型间呈现系统性差异。

Conclusion: 推理一致性扫描是一种可复用的方法，能够在不干预的情况下从评估记录中检测AI推理的逻辑一致性问题，为AI安全评估提供新的工具。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning is often unfaithful: a model's stated reasoning does not reliably reflect the process that produced its output. Detecting unfaithfulness, though, requires controlled experimental interventions, which cannot be applied to evaluation transcripts after the fact. We turn instead to a more tractable question that has received less attention: whether the stated reasoning is logically consistent with the answer it accompanies. Unlike faithfulness, consistency can be assessed from a transcript alone, with no intervention. We introduce reasoning consistency scanning, a reusable method for detecting this property in AI safety evaluation transcripts. Our contributions are fourfold. First, we formalize reasoning consistency as distinct from faithfulness and define a six-subtype taxonomy of inconsistency. Second, we build a validated benchmark of 60 transcripts, manually adapted from InstrumentalEval outputs. Third, we implement a working scanner for InspectScout, the first to target this property in safety evaluation transcripts. Fourth, we report results across four generator models and three evaluations from inspect_evals, showing that reasoning inconsistency is present, detectable, and varies systematically across both models and task types.

</details>


### [97] [From Atomic Actions to Standard Operating Procedures: Iterative Tool Optimization for Self-Evolving LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07321)
*Haipeng Ding,Yuexiang Xie,Zhewei Wei,Yaliang Li,Bolin Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EvoSOP框架，让LLM代理通过将原子操作合成为可复用的标准操作流程（SOP）实现自我进化，实验表明该方法能显著提升任务成功率并减少交互轮次。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代理框架依赖静态的细粒度原子工具集，导致代理需为每个重复工作流重新设计底层逻辑，增加了推理开销和失败率。

Method: 提出EvoSOP框架，通过从执行轨迹中提取SOP，并经过构建、合并、评估和剪枝的系统化生命周期，迭代优化工具集，使代理能够自我进化。

Result: 实验表明EvoSOP在显著提升任务成功率的同时，大幅减少了交互轮次，相比基线方法表现更优。

Conclusion: 迭代式工具优化能够催生可靠高效的工具使用模式，为开发自我进化的代理提供了可扩展的路径。

Abstract: Tool utilization enables Large Language Model (LLM) agents to interact with the real world and resolve complex tasks. However, existing agent frameworks predominantly rely on static toolsets composed of granular atomic actions (e.g., basic file I/O or single-turn search), which forces agents to reinvent low-level logic for every recurring workflow, leading to increased reasoning overhead and failure rates. In this study, we propose that agents can achieve self-evolution by synthesizing these atomic actions into reusable Standard Operating Procedures (SOPs), which function as callable higher-order tools that encapsulate multi-step logic. We further introduce EvoSOP, a framework that empowers agents to extract SOPs from execution trajectories and iteratively optimize the toolset through a systematic lifecycle of construction, merging, evaluation, and pruning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EvoSOP significantly boosts task success rates while substantially reducing the number of interaction rounds compared to baselines. Our analysis also reveals that iterative tool optimization fosters reliable and efficient tool-use patterns, providing a scalable pathway for the development of self-evolving agents.

</details>


### [98] [Physics-Audited Agentic Discovery in Scientific Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07379)
*Diab W. Abueidda,Bilal Ahmed,Panos Pantidis,Mostafa E. Mobasher*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出PA-SciML框架，在智能体科学机器学习中引入物理审计优先的工作流程，通过可验证的物理约束检查替代纯误差评估，在固体力学案例中证明了该方法能筛选出既满足低误差又符合物理定律的替代模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于误差指标（如损失函数）的智能体代理模型选择方法无法保证预测场满足力学中的边界条件、叠加原理、刚度缩放或因果性等物理约束，导致高评分模型可能物理上不可靠。

Method: 提出PA-SciML方法：1) 在搜索前固定评分评估器；2) 推导可审查的机器可检查物理需求；3) 对每个候选模型在输出上进行物理检查；4) 在无参考解的输入范围内搜索高违规案例。同时包含训练前的数值探针和单变量编辑测试。

Result: 在静弹性案例中，PA-SciML选出的代理模型验证误差低于纯误差基线，且两者均通过线性弹性检查；在瞬态弹性动力学案例中，纯误差基线虽然均误差相近但未能通过更严格的因果性检查（对未来载荷做出响应），而PA-SciML选出的模型通过了所有检查。

Conclusion: 物理审计（而非更丰富的聚合评分）是区分候选模型的关键，验证优先的流程能有效识别符合物理规律的代理模型，避免高评分但物理不一致的模型被误用。

Abstract: In agentic scientific machine learning (SciML), large language model (LLM) agents can discover surrogate models and select one by an automated score, typically an error metric. A low error, however, does not establish that the predicted fields satisfy the physics that matter for mechanics, such as boundary conditions, superposition, stiffness scaling, or causality. We introduce Physics-Audited Agentic SciML (PA-SciML), a verification-first workflow for agentic SciML discovery. The workflow fixes a scoring evaluator before search, derives reviewable machine-checkable physics requirements, checks each trained candidate on its outputs, and separately searches prescribed input ranges or measured load-history spans for high-violation cases without reference solution fields. A surrogate is reported as verified only under the stated checks. When enabled, the workflow also adds advisory numerical probes before training and tests one modeling change at a time to record which isolated edits are associated with score gains before reuse. In the reported computational-solid-mechanics numerical examples, the static elasticity run selects a surrogate with lower validation error than the error-only baseline while both selected models pass the common linear-elastic checks. In the transient elastodynamics run, an error-only baseline with similar mean error fails a stricter causality check by responding to future parts of the loading history, while the selected surrogate passes the stated checks. The main distinction is per-candidate physics evidence on predicted fields, not a richer aggregate score.

</details>


### [99] [MIRA-Math: A Benchmark for Minimal Information Requesting and Mathematical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07391)
*Charbel Al Bateh,Samer Saab*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIRA-Math是一个数学推理基准，要求模型在缺少一个关键事实的条件下，通过自然语言请求获取缺失信息，并在有限预算内给出准确答案。该基准包含22个数学家族的2,310个实例，实验表明请求能力和最终答案准确性是可分离的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准要么提供所有必要事实（缺乏交互性），要么混合推理与工具使用、检索等（过于复杂）。作者希望在"缺失单一原子事实"这一诊断性能力上进行精确评估，填补中间空白。

Method: 构建MIRA-Math基准：每个问题缺失一个关键事实，求解器需在有限预算内用自然语言请求缺失信息，固定LLM响应器根据请求匹配程度返回事实或拒绝；整个流程的实例生成、提示规范、验证和答案检查都是确定性的。

Result: 包含22个数学家族（代数、概率、线性系统、离散结构、信号处理、马尔可夫链、电路、插值、数值边值问题）共2,310个实例。实验显示请求成功率和最终答案准确性可分离：模型可能请求到正确事实但后续计算失败，或在获得规范提示前就失败。

Conclusion: MIRA-Math提供了一个可复现的基准，用于评估数学推理中的最小信息请求能力。作者已开源生成器、验证器、提示、运行元数据和数据集文档。

Abstract: Mathematical reasoning benchmarks typically provide all facts needed to solve each problem, while interactive benchmarks often mix reasoning with tools, retrieval, and long-horizon dialogue. We introduce MIRA-Math, a benchmark for a narrower diagnostic capability: solving mathematical problems whose full latent state has a unique answer, but whose solver-facing view is missing exactly one necessary atomic fact. The solver must request the missing information in natural language under a strict budget and then integrate the returned fact into an exact final answer. A fixed constrained LLM responder sees only the dataset-provided atomic fact and must either offer the quoted fact when the request matches it, or decline otherwise. Thus, instance generation, typed hint specifications, validation, and final-answer verification are deterministic, while request metrics are measured under a fixed LLM-mediated responder channel. MIRA-Math contains 2{,}310 generated instances from 22 typed mathematical families spanning algebra, probability, linear systems, discrete structures, signal processing, Markov chains, circuits, interpolation, and numerical boundary-value problems. Experiments across frontier and small models show that request success and final-answer accuracy are separable: models may ask for the right fact yet fail the downstream computation, or fail before obtaining the canonical hint. We release generators, verifiers, prompts, run metadata, and dataset documentation to support reproducible evaluation of minimal information requesting in mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [100] [Agentic Data Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07397)
*Elaine Ang,Chenxi Huang,Georgios Liargkovas,Jerry Liu,Jinhui Liu,Nikos Pagonas,Charlie Summers,Haonan Wang,Jiakai Xu,Tianle Zhou,Yusen Zhang,Zhou Yu,Zhuo Zhang,Tianyi Peng,Kostis Kaffes,Eugene Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出**Agentic Data Environments（代理数据环境）**概念，将数据系统从被动存储转变为主动执行基底，旨在提升自主代理的能力同时确保安全执行，以应对自动化中失败代价高昂的核心挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主代理虽能提升速度与规模，但失败会带来突发且不可逆的代价。核心挑战在于最大化自动化收益的同时，**约束失败的后果**。传统数据库作为被动存储不足以支撑代理在文件、API、应用等更广泛数据环境中的操作。

Method: 重新定义数据系统为**代理的执行基底（execution substrate）**，即Agentic Data Environments，使其既是能力放大器又是安全执行保障的主动参与者。

Result: 尚未给出具体实验结果，属于早期工作（early work）的概念提出阶段。

Conclusion: 将数据系统从**被动状态存储**重构为**安全可靠执行的主动基底**，为解决代理自动化中的安全与能力平衡问题提供了新视角。

Abstract: Autonomous agents promise substantial gains in speed, scale, and labor efficiency, but their failures can impose abrupt and often irreversible costs. The central challenge for agentic automation is therefore to increase the benefits of automation while bounding the consequences of failure.
  While databases remain central to modern computing, agents operate over a broader data environment spanning files, APIs, applications, and system state. In this talk, I will outline early work on Agentic Data Environments -- the execution substrate in which agents operate -- that both amplify agent capabilities and enforce safety guarantees. This perspective reframes data systems from passive stores of state into active substrates for safe, reliable execution.

</details>


### [101] [Reason Less, Verify More: Deterministic Gates Recover a Silent Policy-Violation Failure Mode in Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07405)
*Vikas Reddy,Sumanth Reddy Challaram,Abhishek Basu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM智能体在调用工具时可能出现的“静默违规状态变更”问题，即工具调用在策略层面被禁止，但系统未报错且智能体未察觉。作者在τ²-bench航空域中量化了这一现象（78%的失败源于此类静默错误），并提出了一种轻量级干预方案——确定性预执行门控机制，在写入前检查调用与当前状态。四个门控策略将gpt-4o-mini的成功率从29.6%提升至42.0%，且在多个种子集上可复现。该机制在门控触发的任务上提升显著（+19.2pp），在非触发任务上无显著影响。负对照实验表明，门控仅在策略宽松的工具环境下有效。即便在gpt-5.2上，相同失败模式依然存在，门控仍带来+10.4pp的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在执行任务时可能违反部署策略，却在表面上完成任务。现有工具与环境允许任何格式正确的调用执行，即使该调用对应的状态变更在领域策略中是禁止的。这种“静默错误状态”既不被工具检测，也不被智能体报告，构成了安全漏洞。作者旨在量化此问题并探索轻量级缓解方案。

Method: 作者在τ²-bench航空域构建预算智能体场景，量化静默违规失败率（78%）。提出确定性预执行门控机制——在工具写入前，使用只读检查器对提议的调用和当前状态进行策略合规性验证。设计四个门控组成完整套件，在gpt-4o-mini和gpt-5.2上评估。同时设置负对照实验（零售域和BFCL基准）以界定机制适用范围。

Result: gpt-4o-mini上，门控套件将完整基准成功率从29.6%提升至42.0%（+12.4pp, P=0.0012），在15个独立种子集上复现（+12.3pp, P=0.0008）。效果集中在门控触发的26/50个任务上（+19.2pp），非触发任务无显著变化。gpt-5.2上成功率从61.2%提升至71.6%（+10.4pp, P=0.020）。负对照实验证实门控仅在策略宽松环境中有效。

Conclusion: 确定性门控机制不能保证任务完全成功，但可以在动作边界上确定性阻止一类已知的静默策略违规写入。这是一种有边界、可评估的可靠性提升方法。作者强调这是提示性证据而非核心主张，特别是前沿模型仍存在相同失败模式。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents can violate the very policies they are deployed to enforce while appearing to complete the task successfully. In policy-permissive environments, a tool may execute any well-formed call even when the corresponding state transition is forbidden by domain policy. The result is a silent wrong state (a booking cancelled, a passenger count changed, a claim acted on without verification) that neither the tool nor the agent's self-report exposes.
  We study this failure mode in the $τ^2$-bench airline domain. On a budget agent, 78% of observed failures are silent wrong-state failures with no tool error, and the aggregate failure rate is reproducible across disjoint seeds, not sampling noise. We then evaluate a lightweight intervention: deterministic, read-only pre-execution gates that inspect the proposed call and current state before allowing a write. A four-gate suite raises full-benchmark success from 29.6% to 42.0% on gpt-4o-mini (+12.4pp; paired task-level bootstrap P=0.0012), and the lift reproduces on a disjoint 15-seed set (+12.3pp; P=0.0008).
  The effect is concentrated where the gates fire: on the 26/50 firing tasks, success rises by +19.2pp, while movement on the 24 non-firing tasks does not exclude zero. Two negative controls (a self-enforcing retail domain and BFCL) bound the mechanism: gates help when tools are policy-permissive and add little where tools already self-enforce. As suggestive evidence, not a central claim, the same failure mode persists at the frontier: gpt-5.2 at default reasoning still attempts policy-violating writes, and the same suite improves success from 61.2% to 71.6% (+10.4pp; P=0.020; n=5, no replication). The contribution is a bounded evaluation and reliability result: deterministic gates do not guarantee task success, but they can deterministically prevent a known class of silent policy-violating writes at the action boundary.

</details>


### [102] [InductWave: Inductive Multi-Hop Logical Query Answering on Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07422)
*Mayank Kharbanda,Michael Cochez,Rajiv Ratn Shah,Raghava Mutharaju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: InductWave是一种基于小波的归纳式嵌入方法，用于知识图谱上的逻辑多跳查询回答，能够在训练图节点更少的情况下，以更少的消息传递层数达到或超越现有基线模型性能，并支持大型图谱（如Wiki-KG）的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有知识图谱逻辑查询方法大多基于传递推理，无法处理训练时未见过的实体；现实中由于资源稀缺，难以在大规模KG上训练包含所有节点的模型，因此需要一种归纳式、资源高效的查询推理方法。

Method: 提出InductWave，一种基于小波变换的归纳式嵌入方法，利用小波在图上进行消息传递，以更少的层数实现有效的归纳推理，支持对大图进行逻辑查询回答。

Result: 在FB15k-237数据集上，InductWave在多数情况下以75%的层数超越所有基线模型，并以一半的层数达到与基线相当的性能；此外能在Wiki-KG等大规模图谱上运行。

Conclusion: InductWave通过小波归纳嵌入有效降低了资源需求，同时保持甚至提升了逻辑查询回答的性能，适用于资源受限场景下的大规模知识图谱推理。

Abstract: Logical Multi-Hop Query Answering over Knowledge Graphs (KGs) can be formulated as querying, with an implicit completeness assumption. Current works mainly focus on Existential First Order Logic (EFO) queries. These EFO queries contain conjunction, disjunction, and negation operators. Most existing works employ transductive reasoning, meaning they are not capable of reasoning over entities unseen during training. In the real world, there is a resource scarcity, and we cannot train a model with all the nodes of a large KG. Hence, we propose InductWave, a wavelet-based inductive embedding method for logical query answering on large KGs. Here, the training graph consists of fewer nodes than the test graph. Our model performs on par with the baseline models while having half the number of message-passing layers. It outperforms all of them in most cases, with 75% of the layers. These fewer resource requirements enable us to evaluate InductWave on massive graphs, such as Wiki-KG. We test our model using extensive experiments across varying train-test graph proportions of the FB15k-(237) dataset, comparing it with the state-of-the-art models. The code and datasets for the model are available at https://github.com/kracr/inductwave/.

</details>


### [103] [The Blind Curator: How a Biased Judge Silently Disables Skill Retirement in Self-Evolving Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07436)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Ziyuan Li,Wei Qiu,Bing Zhu,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了自进化智能体中，当使用有偏的LLM裁判（而非无偏奖励）时，“技能退役”机制会静默失效：假阳性偏差（将失败误判为成功）会使得基于贡献度的技能退役越过一个无法通过增加数据克服的阈值而失效，且这种机制失效在下游指标上不可见，构成一个行为安全性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能退役机制假设奖励是无偏的，但参考无关任务迫使使用LLM作为裁判，而LLM裁判存在偏差。作者想探究这种偏差对技能退役机制的影响，尤其是它是否会导致系统静默退化而无法被察觉。

Method: 通过损坏奖励分析（corrupted-reward analysis）进行理论刻画，并在参考无关的报告写作测试平台上进行行为研究，使用代码生成交叉检查。通过将确定性奖励上注入噪声来隔离因果通道，区分真正的退役与帽驱逐（cap-eviction churn）。

Result: 对称噪声不影响退役机制，但假阳性偏差（false-pass bias）会使基于贡献的退役在超过某个尖锐阈值后完全失效，且数据量无法弥补。这种机制失败在不同领域和失败率下普遍存在，只有接近零假阳性率的验证器类评分者才能幸免。然而下游评估质量仅在相同腐败同时影响技能合成时才会下降，否则保持稳定，导致被禁用的管理者在聚合指标中不显现。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献是一个行为安全性结果而非性能结果。提出了一个廉价的缺陷注入审计方法，可以在部署前判断裁判是否处于安全阈值内。

Abstract: A self-evolving agent retires its bad skills by watching them fail, so what happens when the judge cannot see the failures? Skill retirement is the structural constraint that keeps a growing library from drifting below the no-skill baseline, but its guarantee assumes an unbiased reward, which is false for the LLM judges that reference-free tasks force upon us. We show that a biased judge does not merely add noise; it \emph{silently switches off the curator}. We make this precise with a corrupted-reward analysis and, isolating the causal channel by injecting corruption on top of a deterministic reward, a behavioral study on a reference-free report-writing testbed with a code-generation cross-check. Symmetric noise leaves retirement intact, but \emph{false-pass} bias (failures slipping through as passes) disables contribution-based retirement past a sharp threshold that no amount of data can cross. Separating genuine retirement from cap-eviction churn shows this \emph{mechanism} failure is universal, holding across domains and failure rates and sparing only near-zero-false-pass, verifier-like graders. The downstream \emph{outcome}, though, is regime-dependent: eval quality degrades only where the same corruption also starves skill synthesis, and otherwise holds steady, so the disabled curator is \emph{silent}, surfacing in no aggregate metric. The contribution is a behavioral safety result, not a performance one. A cheap defect-injection audit then tells an operator, before deployment, which side of the threshold their judge occupies.

</details>


### [104] [SpaCellAgent: A Self-Evolving LLM-Based Multi-Agent Framework for Trajectory Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07467)
*Songhan Wang,Haoang Chi,He Li,Zhiheng Zhang,Jiayan Yuan,Cheems Wang,Hao Peng,Xinwang Liu,Wenjing Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SpaCellAgent 是一个基于大语言模型的多智能体框架，能够自动化完成从自然语言指令到空间单细胞轨迹推断的端到端分析流程，在6个异构数据集上实现了超过40%的分析效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有轨迹推断（TI）方法需要大量人工干预且工具异构性强，缺乏统一自动化流程，为高效分析设置了显著障碍。

Method: 提出 SpaCellAgent，包含三个核心模块：多智能体架构（战略工作流规划）、动态工具编排引擎（自适应算法选择）和自进化模块（通过反馈迭代优化性能）。

Result: 在6个涵盖复杂时空发育轨迹、多种测序平台和空间组织结构的异构数据集上，SpaCellAgent 在保持与专家相当性能的同时，分析效率提升超过40%。

Conclusion: SpaCellAgent 将自然语言规范自动转化为优化分析工作流，实现了高级时空建模的民主化，建立了可扩展的、智能体驱动的计算生物学新范式。

Abstract: Spatial and Single-cell transcriptomics are transformative in deciphering cellular dynamics. As the fundamental paradigm for reconstructing cell developmental paths, trajectory inference (TI) is critical. However, existing methods require extensive manual intervention and proficiency in heterogeneous tools, posing a significant barrier to efficient TI analysis. To bridge this gap, we propose SpaCellAgent, an autonomous large language model (LLM) multi-agent framework that automates end-to-end spatiotemporal analysis and narrative generation. SpaCellAgent utilizes a multi-agent architecture for strategic workflow planning, a dynamic tool-orchestration engine for adaptive algorithm selection, and a self-evolution module that iteratively refines performance through feedback. We evaluate SpaCellAgent on six heterogeneous datasets encompassing complex temporal developmental trajectories, diverse sequencing platforms, and spatially-resolved tissue architectures. SpaCellAgent consistently demonstrates over 40\% improvement in analytical efficiency while maintaining expert-aligned performance. By converting natural language specifications into optimized analytical workflows and fully automating the pipeline, SpaCellAgent democratizes advanced spatiotemporal modeling and establishes a scalable, agent-driven paradigm for computational biology. The code and materials are available at https://github.com/LittleXH-shw/SpaCellAgent.

</details>


### [105] [Search, Fail, Recover: A Training Framework for Correction-Aware Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07492)
*Dmitry Beresnev,Vladimir Makharev,Roman Khalikov,Ivan Oseledets,Petr Anokhin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Pyligent是一个训练和推理框架，通过显式监督"继续/完成/回溯"三种动作，让模型学会在推理失败时回溯并恢复，在多个结构化推理任务上显著优于纯监督微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
